## Radar Systems

### Pulse Compression Search Radar (`LW_Mk2`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/LW_Mk2.png -->

Pulse-Compression is a signal processing technique that involves modulating a transmitted radar pulse and then correlating the returned signal with the pulse that was transmitted, allowing radar systems to increase their range resolution or their signal-to-noise ratio by shortening the duration of echoes. Increasing their signal-to-noise ratio and range resolution allows radars to detect returns on objects from further away, increasing the range at which they can detect objects.  
Increases the operating range of long-wave search radars.

*Level 1, cost 5, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 2*

**Requires:** [Pulse Doppler Radar](#pulse-doppler-radar-radar_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Phased Array Search Radar](#phased-array-search-radar-lw_mk3).

**Improves:**

- [Search Radar (STD Long Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#search-radar-std-long-wave-std-long-wave):
    - `range` = 450
    - `maxheight` = 250
- [Search Radar (Fixed Long Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#search-radar-fixed-long-wave-fixed-long-wave):
    - `range` = 725
    - `maxheight` = 250
- [Ground Detection](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#ground-detection-awacs-long-wave): `range` = 450
- [Air Detection](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#air-detection-awacs-air-detection): `range` = 950
- [Submarine Radar Mast](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#submarine-radar-mast-submarine-radar-mast):
    - `range` = 225
    - `maxheight` = 250

### Pulse Doppler Tracking Radar (`SW_Mk2`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SW_Mk2.png -->

The ability for Doppler Radar to track an object's range and velocity is crucial for many tracking and targeting systems. Being able to accurately gauge an object's movement in space makes it considerably easier to keep track of where it is and by extension, target it with weapons and reliably hit it.  
Increases the operating range of targeting radars.

*Level 1, cost 5, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 2*

**Requires:** [Pulse Doppler Radar](#pulse-doppler-radar-radar_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Phased Array Tracking Radar](#phased-array-tracking-radar-sw_mk3).

**Improves:**

- [AA Gun](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#aa-gun-aa-gun-old):
    - `range` = 140
    - `precision` = 9
- [Targeting Radar (STD Short Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#targeting-radar-std-short-wave-std-short-wave):
    - `range` = 225
    - `maxheight` = 250
- [Targeting Radar (Fixed Short Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#targeting-radar-fixed-short-wave-fixed-short-wave):
    - `range` = 375
    - `maxheight` = 250
- [Ground Targeting](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#ground-targeting-awacs-short-wave): `range` = 350
- [SW Targeting Radar](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#sw-targeting-radar-interceptor-short-wave):
    - `range` = 325
    - `maxheight` = 250
- [Air Targeting](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#air-targeting-awacs-air-targeting): `range` = 700

### Object Cataloging Space Radar (`Space_radar`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPACE_RADAR_1.png -->

Space-facing radars were originally developed as a scientific endeavor to observe and monitor activity in the world outside of Earth's atmosphere, but after it became apparent that man-made satellites could have military applications (which didn't take very long at all) there was a sudden strategic need for satellite tracking capabilities. Early outer space monitoring worked by identifying orbiting objects, cataloging their characteristics and then modelling their predicted orbits, rather than actively tracking them at all times. As a result, the arrival of new objects or changes in the orbits of cataloged assets might go unnoticed for days, weeks or even months before a change was noted. Despite the shortcomings of this system, being aware of enemy activity in space is a critical strategic need.  
Allows for the construction of Space Radars, which can detect enemy satellites.

*Level 1, cost 15, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 2*

**Requires:** [Pulse Doppler Radar](#pulse-doppler-radar-radar_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Actively Tracked Space Radar](#actively-tracked-space-radar-space_radar_mk2), [Nuclear Anti-Satellite Weapons](#nuclear-anti-satellite-weapons-nuke_asat), [Laser Defence Satellites](#laser-defence-satellites-satellite_defence_lasers).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Space Radar](A1-Catalog-Units.md#space-radar-space_radar) (`Space_radar`)
- Sensor [Overhorizon Radar (Small Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-small-over-horizon-small_over_horizon) (`Small_Over_Horizon`)
- Sensor [Space radar (Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-space-radar-space_radar) (`Space_Radar`)
- Sensor [Space radar (Naval Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-naval-space-radar-naval-space_radar) (`Naval Space_Radar`)
- Sensor [Space radar (Satellite Mini Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-satellite-mini-space-radar-satellite_mini_space_radar) (`Satellite_Mini_Space_Radar`)

### Over-The-Horizon Warning Radar (`Over_horizon_radar`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/Over_horizon_radar.png -->

The blinding speed and extreme firing ranges of ballistic missiles means that giving warning of an incoming attack isn't tenable with conventional radar, which led to the development of Over-The-Horizon (OTH) radars. Usually, radar systems can only detect objects within their line of sight, but the ability to bounce signals off of the ionosphere makes it possible for exceptionally large and powerful radar stations to detect targets from over the horizon, and by extension, give early warning of incoming ballistic missile threats.  
Early warning is crucial for missile defence systems to operate, as well as to ensure that friendly nuclear forces have time to respond before they're destroyed. Make sure you have an early warning system active if you want to survive a first strike!

*Level 1, cost 20, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 2*

**Requires:** [Pulse Doppler Radar](#pulse-doppler-radar-radar_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Advanced Early Warning Radar](#advanced-early-warning-radar-over_horizon_radar_mk2).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Over-The-Horizon Radar](A1-Catalog-Units.md#over-the-horizon-radar-over_horizon_radar) (`Over_Horizon_radar`)
- Sensor [Overhorizon Radar (Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-over-horizon-over_horizon) (`Over_Horizon`)
- Sensor [Overhorizon Radar (Naval Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-naval-over-horizon-naval_over_horizon) (`Naval_Over_Horizon`)
- Sensor [Overhorizon Radar (Naval Small Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-naval-small-over-horizon-naval_small_over_horizon) (`Naval_Small_Over_Horizon`)

### Pulse Doppler Radar (`Radar_Mk2`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/RADAR_2.png -->

A pulse-doppler radar combines pulse and continuous-wave technology to determine the distance, direction and speed of objects. Being able to accurately determine the velocity and distance of an object is useful for developing a viable firing solution and hitting a target. As a result, pulse-doppler radar technology is extremely useful when used in search, tracking and targeting radars, and will make it easier to successfully find, track and hit airborne threats.  
Unlocks mobile radar sites. Allows for the development of improved search and targeting radars, as well as space-facing radar and over-the-horizon radar.

*Level 1, cost 20, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 1*

**Requires:** nothing.

**Leads to:** [Object Cataloging Space Radar](#object-cataloging-space-radar-space_radar), [Over-The-Horizon Warning Radar](#over-the-horizon-warning-radar-over_horizon_radar), [Pulse Compression Search Radar](#pulse-compression-search-radar-lw_mk2), [Pulse Doppler Tracking Radar](#pulse-doppler-tracking-radar-sw_mk2), [Phased Array Radar](#phased-array-radar-radar_mk3).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Short-Wave Radar Vehicle](A1-Catalog-Units.md#short-wave-radar-vehicle-mobile_sw_radar) (`Mobile_SW_Radar`)

**Improves:**

- [Radar Site](A1-Catalog-Units.md#radar-site-fixed_lw_radar): new appearance

### Phased Array Search Radar (`LW_Mk3`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/LW_Mk3.png -->

Search radars that can actively move their antennae will be more effective at rapidly scanning the battlespace for enemy contacts. Although mechanically rotating is still very effective on small, fast moving radars for wide sweeps, phased array systems are useful for sweeping in multiple planes, or on large radars that are difficult to physically rotate or angle.  
Increases the operating range of long-wave search radars.

*Level 2, cost 10, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 3*

**Requires:** [Pulse Compression Search Radar](#pulse-compression-search-radar-lw_mk2), [Phased Array Radar](#phased-array-radar-radar_mk3).

**Leads to:** [AESA Search Radar](#aesa-search-radar-lw_mk4).

**Improves:**

- [Search Radar (STD Long Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#search-radar-std-long-wave-std-long-wave):
    - `range` = 500
    - `maxheight` = 300
- [Search Radar (Fixed Long Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#search-radar-fixed-long-wave-fixed-long-wave):
    - `range` = 850
    - `maxheight` = 300
- [Ground Detection](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#ground-detection-awacs-long-wave): `range` = 500
- [Air Detection](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#air-detection-awacs-air-detection): `range` = 1 100
- [Submarine Radar Mast](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#submarine-radar-mast-submarine-radar-mast):
    - `range` = 250
    - `maxheight` = 300

### Phased Array Tracking Radar (`SW_Mk3`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SW_Mk3.png -->

Implementing phased array technology into a tracking and targeting radar allows for finer beam adjustments without mechanically moving the radar system, and by extension, more consistent tracking with better accuracy. As a result, tracking and targeting radars will be more effective.  
Increases the operating range of targeting radars.

*Level 2, cost 10, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 3*

**Requires:** [Pulse Doppler Tracking Radar](#pulse-doppler-tracking-radar-sw_mk2), [Phased Array Radar](#phased-array-radar-radar_mk3).

**Leads to:** [AESA Targeting Radar](#aesa-targeting-radar-sw_mk4).

**Improves:**

- [AA Gun](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#aa-gun-aa-gun-old):
    - `range` = 160
    - `precision` = 8
- [Targeting Radar (STD Short Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#targeting-radar-std-short-wave-std-short-wave):
    - `range` = 250
    - `maxheight` = 300
- [Targeting Radar (Fixed Short Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#targeting-radar-fixed-short-wave-fixed-short-wave):
    - `range` = 450
    - `maxheight` = 300
- [Ground Targeting](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#ground-targeting-awacs-short-wave): `range` = 400
- [SW Targeting Radar](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#sw-targeting-radar-interceptor-short-wave):
    - `range` = 400
    - `maxheight` = 300
- [Air Targeting](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#air-targeting-awacs-air-targeting): `range` = 825

### Actively Tracked Space Radar (`Space_radar_Mk2`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPACE_RADAR_2.png -->

The shortcomings with early space object cataloging had to be addressed, and as a result, extremely high-frequency radars would be developed to actively track orbiting objects as they passed overhead. The data collected by these radars would then be compiled into a central computer that tracked the locations and orbits of all known spaceborne objects, meaning that space-tracking could now happen in real time and with a much higher degree of diligence. The development of these new space radars themselves would also make it much easier to identify artificial objects in space.  
Increases the range of space radars.

*Level 2, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 3*

**Requires:** [Object Cataloging Space Radar](#object-cataloging-space-radar-space_radar).

**Leads to:** [High-Fidelity ASAT Space Radar](#high-fidelity-asat-space-radar-space_radar_mk3).

**Improves:**

- [Space radar (Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-space-radar-space_radar): `range` = 2 500
- [Space radar (Naval Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-naval-space-radar-naval-space_radar): `range` = 625
- [Space radar (Satellite Mini Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-satellite-mini-space-radar-satellite_mini_space_radar): `range` = 1 250

### Phased Array Radar (`Radar_Mk3`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/RADAR_3.png -->

Phased-Array radars can electronically steer the direction of their radar transmission in order to more efficiently scan and gather information about their targets. Directing the radar beam itself is much quicker than physically moving the radar antenna, which makes it easier to sweep and search for targets. Being able to actively and precisely focus radar on a single target makes it easier to develop firing solutions from further away, and concurrent improvements in computing technology will make it possible to track multiple targets at once with greater precision.  
Allows for the development of improved search and targeting radars.

*Level 2, cost 35, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 2*

**Requires:** [Pulse Doppler Radar](#pulse-doppler-radar-radar_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Phased Array Search Radar](#phased-array-search-radar-lw_mk3), [Phased Array Tracking Radar](#phased-array-tracking-radar-sw_mk3), [AESA Radar](#aesa-radar-radar_mk4).

### Advanced Early Warning Radar (`Over_horizon_radar_Mk2`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/Over_horizon_radar_Mk2.png -->

Ground-based OTH radars use an extreme amount of power and are expensive to operate, but are still valuable for the sole purpose of detecting incoming ballistic missiles. As airborne radars seen on AEW&C aircraft (better known as AWACS) began to proliferate, the role of long-range missile detection began to shift to them, and the capability to identify and track ballistic missiles from great distances became a major consideration. Advancements in signal processing and electronic miniaturization would increase the potential for OTH radar sites to detect incoming missiles from further away, while also granting dedicated airborne radar systems the ability to partially take over the same responsibility.  
Increases the operating range of OTH radar. Allows AWACS to detect ballistic missiles from long range.

*Level 3, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 5*

**Requires:** [Over-The-Horizon Warning Radar](#over-the-horizon-warning-radar-over_horizon_radar).

**Unlocks:**

- Sensor [Overhorizon Radar (AWACS Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-awacs-over-horizon-awacs_over_horizon) (`AWACS_Over_Horizon`)

**Improves:**

- [Overhorizon Radar (Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-over-horizon-over_horizon): `range` = 6 000
- [Overhorizon Radar (AWACS Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-awacs-over-horizon-awacs_over_horizon): `range` = 2 000
- [Overhorizon Radar (Naval Over Horizon)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#overhorizon-radar-naval-over-horizon-naval_over_horizon): `range` = 1 500

### AESA Search Radar (`LW_Mk4`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/LW_Mk4.png -->

Active Electronically Scanned Array (AESA) search radars use an array of individually-directable antennae that can each transmit on a different frequency at once in order to rapidly scan a battlespace for contacts. Because every radar beam can point in a different direction, it's possible to effectively 'look everywhere at once' on a wide range of frequencies, making it much more difficult for any hostile assets to go undetected. As a result, search radars can benefit greatly from AESA technology.  
Increases the operating range of long-wave search radars.

*Level 5, cost 15, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 6*

**Requires:** [Phased Array Search Radar](#phased-array-search-radar-lw_mk3), [AESA Radar](#aesa-radar-radar_mk4).

**Leads to:** [Experimental LW Search Radar](#experimental-lw-search-radar-lw_mk5).

**Improves:**

- [Search Radar (STD Long Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#search-radar-std-long-wave-std-long-wave):
    - `range` = 600
    - `maxheight` = 400
- [Search Radar (Fixed Long Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#search-radar-fixed-long-wave-fixed-long-wave):
    - `range` = 1 000
    - `maxheight` = 400
- [Ground Detection](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#ground-detection-awacs-long-wave): `range` = 600
- [Air Detection](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#air-detection-awacs-air-detection): `range` = 1 300
- [Submarine Radar Mast](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#submarine-radar-mast-submarine-radar-mast):
    - `range` = 300
    - `maxheight` = 400

### AESA Targeting Radar (`SW_Mk4`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SW_Mk4.png -->

Active Electronically Scanned Array (AESA) targeting radars can rapidly change between frequencies and scan on multiple frequencies at once, allowing them to pick up more information on their targets and better discern their range and motion. As a result, AESA targeting radars are extremely effective, and even have the potential to counteract stealth technology to an extent.  
Increases the operating range of targeting radars.

*Level 5, cost 15, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 6*

**Requires:** [Phased Array Tracking Radar](#phased-array-tracking-radar-sw_mk3), [AESA Radar](#aesa-radar-radar_mk4).

**Leads to:** [Experimental Tracking Radars](#experimental-tracking-radars-sw_mk5).

**Improves:**

- [AA Gun](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#aa-gun-aa-gun-old):

    - `precision` = 7
- [Targeting Radar (STD Short Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#targeting-radar-std-short-wave-std-short-wave):
    - `range` = 300
    - `maxheight` = 400
- [Targeting Radar (Fixed Short Wave)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#targeting-radar-fixed-short-wave-fixed-short-wave):
    - `range` = 550
    - `maxheight` = 400
- [Ground Targeting](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#ground-targeting-awacs-short-wave): `range` = 500
- [SW Targeting Radar](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#sw-targeting-radar-interceptor-short-wave):
    - `range` = 500
    - `maxheight` = 400
- [Air Targeting](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#air-targeting-awacs-air-targeting): `range` = 975

### High-Fidelity ASAT Space Radar (`Space_radar_Mk3`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPACE_RADAR_3.png -->

As space transformed from a peaceful uncontested area into a highly important battlespace, being able to track military satellites became a key concern. New high-definition space radars would be developed specifically to identify enemy space assets and provide targeting data for ASAT weapons, rather than just keeping tabs on their positions and tracking space junk. Although their operation would be similar to previous space-tracking radars, their performance would be increased to ensure that any new unannounced satellites would be detected sooner, and that any maneuvers or orbital changes would be identified more easily.  
Further increases the range of space radars.

*Level 5, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 6*

**Requires:** [Actively Tracked Space Radar](#actively-tracked-space-radar-space_radar_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Counter-Stealth Space Radar](#counter-stealth-space-radar-space_radar_mk4).

**Improves:**

- [Space radar (Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-space-radar-space_radar): `range` = 3 000
- [Space radar (Naval Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-naval-space-radar-naval-space_radar): `range` = 750
- [Space radar (Satellite Mini Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-satellite-mini-space-radar-satellite_mini_space_radar): `range` = 1 500

### AESA Radar (`Radar_Mk4`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/RADAR_4.png -->

An Active Electronically Scanned Array (AESA) radar uses phased array technology to direct radar beams electronically, but takes the concept further by individually moving each individual antenna in the array. Having this level of control allows each antenna to broadcast on a different frequency in a different direction, allowing it to gather much more information at once. AESA radar is also less conspicuous because its transmissions are spread over a wider range of frequencies. The multi-frequency capabilities of AESA radar make it very attractive for tracking and targeting radars, as it makes the radar harder to defeat with countermeasures and provides highly accurate targeting information quickly.  
Allows for the development of improved search and targeting radars.

*Level 5, cost 50, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 5*

**Requires:** [Phased Array Radar](#phased-array-radar-radar_mk3).

**Leads to:** [AESA Search Radar](#aesa-search-radar-lw_mk4), [AESA Targeting Radar](#aesa-targeting-radar-sw_mk4), [Advanced AESA Jammers](#advanced-aesa-jammers-jammers_mk4), [Novel Wide-Spectrum Radar Concepts](#novel-wide-spectrum-radar-concepts-radar_mk5).

**Improves:**

- [Radar Site](A1-Catalog-Units.md#radar-site-fixed_lw_radar): new appearance

### Experimental LW Search Radar (`LW_Mk5`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/LW_Mk5.png -->

Next-generation search radars that can scan on a wide range of frequencies and filter out genuine targets in the presence of ambient noise will be crucial in detecting hostile units from further away. Scanning on a wide frequency will ensure that any contacts will generate a radar return in some portion of the spectrum, and improved transmission strength and noise filtering will help ensure that the return is strong enough to be detected.  
Increases the operating range of long-wave search radars.

*Level 8, cost 20, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 9*

**Requires:** [AESA Search Radar](#aesa-search-radar-lw_mk4), [Novel Wide-Spectrum Radar Concepts](#novel-wide-spectrum-radar-concepts-radar_mk5).

**Leads to:** [Quantum Radar](#quantum-radar-radar_mk6).

### Experimental Tracking Radars (`SW_Mk5`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SW_Mk5.png -->

Emerging high-power radar technology that can track on a very wide spectrum has the potential to improve the performance of tracking and targeting radars, as transmitting on a variety of frequencies ensures that any significant objects will be detected in one band of the spectrum or another, while improved transmission strength and signal processing algorithms will also help ensure that the radar returns are strong and high-resolution enough to form targeting solutions.  
Increases the operating range of targeting radars.

*Level 8, cost 20, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 9*

### Novel Wide-Spectrum Radar Concepts (`Radar_Mk5`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/RADAR_5.png -->

Cutting-edge radar technology will have to be even more powerful, and capable of transmitting and receiving from a wide range of frequencies in order to defeat the emerging and proliferating threat posed by stealth assets. Improved radar transmitters and signal processors will be needed to saturate a target with radar and then discern between ambient noise and the threat, and then focus in on that threat enough that it can be targeted with weapons. This level of performance will have to be achievable in a system that is small enough to be carried by tactical systems like combat aircraft and mobile SAM complexes, which will prove to be a challenging engineering feat.  
Allows for the development of improved search and targeting radars.

*Level 8, cost 75, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 8*

**Requires:** [AESA Radar](#aesa-radar-radar_mk4).

**Leads to:** [Experimental LW Search Radar](#experimental-lw-search-radar-lw_mk5), [Experimental Tracking Radars](#experimental-tracking-radars-sw_mk5), [Quantum Radar](#quantum-radar-radar_mk6).

### Counter-Stealth Space Radar (`Space_radar_Mk4`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPACE_RADAR_4.png -->

The increasing importance of controlling outer space and the advent of advanced military satellites, including some with stealth characteristics, creates a demand for ever-increasing space-tracking capabilities. Next-generation, high-resolution space radars with massive transmission power and wide-spectrum frequency scanning will be necessary to track stealth satellites, as well as discern between the growing amount of space junk and genuine contacts. Likewise, the computing centers that process all of the incoming data and turn it into actionable intelligence will also have to be well-prepared for the task of actively monitoring the thousands of man-made objects in space.  
Further increases the range of space radars. Nullifies the effects of basic stealth technology on enemy satellites.

*Level 9, cost 45, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 10*

**Requires:** [High-Fidelity ASAT Space Radar](#high-fidelity-asat-space-radar-space_radar_mk3).

- [Space radar (Naval Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-naval-space-radar-naval-space_radar): `range` = 800
- [Space radar (Satellite Mini Space Radar)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#space-radar-satellite-mini-space-radar-satellite_mini_space_radar): `range` = 1 625

### Quantum Radar (`Radar_Mk6`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/RADAR_6.png -->

Quantum radar is a speculative means of remote-sensing that uses quantum entanglement to detect targets. Although the operation of hypothetical quantum radars is quite complex, the general principle is that it uses two quantumly entangled beams of microwave energy to determine the presence of a target. One beam, the 'signal beam' is sent outwards to search for a target while the other 'idler' beam is observed. If the signal beam interacts with a target, then the idler beam will be affected due to the quantum entanglement, and observing changes in the idler beam can be used to determine what the signal beam has interacted with, thus identifying the location and nature of a target. In practical terms, quantum radar is much better at discerning targets from ambient noise than regular radars, making it much harder to jam, and has been cited as a possible means of defeating stealth technology. Because of the technical complexities and challenges with quantum radar, they are only available for high-cost, specialized platforms.  
Quantum radar operates similarly to conventional targeting radar. It does not necessarily have a longer range, but it is not affected by stealth technology or jammers.  
Adds quantum radar to fixed and mobile radar sites, surface warships, and AWACS.

*Level 10, cost 120, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 99*

**Requires:** [Experimental LW Search Radar](#experimental-lw-search-radar-lw_mk5), [Experimental Tracking Radars](#experimental-tracking-radars-sw_mk5), [Novel Wide-Spectrum Radar Concepts](#novel-wide-spectrum-radar-concepts-radar_mk5).

**Unlocks:**

- Sensor [High-Power Quantum Radar](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#high-power-quantum-radar-quantum-radar-strong) (`Quantum Radar Strong`)
- Sensor [Quantum Radar](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#quantum-radar-quantum-radar-weak) (`Quantum Radar Weak`)

**Improves:**

- [High-Power Quantum Radar](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#high-power-quantum-radar-quantum-radar-strong), [Quantum Radar](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#quantum-radar-quantum-radar-weak): `range` ×1

## Electronic Warfare

### Mobile Jammers (`Jammers`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/EWF1_3.png -->

Radio jamming is an electronic warfare technique where the operation of a radio transmitter or receiver is deliberately interfered with by an opposing transmitter, also known as a jammer. There are multiple ways to jam a radio (and by extension, radar) system, but the most basic ones involve overwhelming the receiver by broadcasting random noise so that it can’t properly interpret the information it’s meant to, whether that’s radio communications or radar targeting information or something else. The concept of radio and radar jamming can be traced back to the early days of radar, but making a jammer sophisticated enough to be competitive against military hardware, as well as compact and powerful enough to be mobile and effective at ranges that are useful in warfare, requires a fair amount of effort.  
Unlocks mobile jammers, which reduces the effective range of enemy radars when they are within range.

*Level 1, cost 15, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 1*

**Requires:** nothing.

**Leads to:** [SEAD/EW Aircraft](#seadew-aircraft-jammers_mk2).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Jamming Vehicle](A1-Catalog-Units.md#jamming-vehicle-ew_mobile) (`EW_MOBILE`)

### Chaff and Flare Countermeasures (`Electronic_Counters_Mk1`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/ECM_1.png -->

The development of the first homing missiles created an instant demand for protection against them. Given the primitive guidance systems of these new threats, the most practical and readily developable solution to counteract them was to create ways to confuse their guidance systems and make them miss their targets. Radar-reflecting chaff was developed shortly after the proliferation of radar as a means of giving off confusing and misleading radar returns, and was quickly adapted into a countermeasure against radar-seeking missiles once they became prominent. Infrared decoys were also rapidly developed as a way to confuse heatseeking missiles by giving off multiple false heat-signatures. By misleading and confusing the guidance systems of radar and heatseeking weapons, aircraft and surface ships would improve their chances of survival by minimizing their chances of being hit, or causing the weapons to explode at the wrong time or in the wrong place and do minimal damage.  
Passively reduces the accuracy and damage done by enemy AShMs, SAMs and AAMs.

*Level 2, cost 15, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 2*

**Requires:** nothing.

**Leads to:** [Active Missile Interference](#active-missile-interference-electronic_counters_mk2).

### SEAD/EW Aircraft (`Jammers_Mk2`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/EWF2.png -->

SEAD, which stands for Suppression of Enemy Air Defences, is a strategy and activity that seeks to undermine the effectiveness of enemy air defence networks by destroying radars and SAM sites as well as diminishing their ability to operate, both through electronic attack (i.e. jamming) and intimidation. Early attempts at this using aircraft were highly dangerous and often done on an ad-hoc basis, but the development of specialized aircraft and weapons would make it more feasible. Modified fighter and bomber aircraft equipped with electronic warfare suites and long-range anti-radiation missiles, which automatically home in on radar transmissions from SAMs and radar sites, were brought into service as a means of threatening enemy SAM capabilities from the air, making it far easier for friendly aircraft to operate. Needless to say, attacking anti-air sites with aircraft is a very dangerous mission, and EW aircraft are expensive and valuable assets, but effective SEAD operations can make it far easier to secure air superiority.  
Unlocks electronic warfare aircraft, which can be launched from carriers and specialized airbases. Electronic warfare aircraft are expensive and vulnerable to enemy fighters, but have strong radars, jamming equipment and missiles specially designed to destroy radars and SAM sites.

*Level 2, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 3*

**Requires:** [Mobile Jammers](#mobile-jammers-jammers).

**Leads to:** [DRFM Jamming Systems](#drfm-jamming-systems-jammers_mk3), [Stealth Technology](#stealth-technology-stealth_technology).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [EW Aircraft](A1-Catalog-Units.md#ew-aircraft-ew_aircraft) (`EW_Aircraft`)
- Weapon [Anti-Radar Missile](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#anti-radar-missile-anti-radiation-missile) (`Anti-Radiation Missile`)

**Improves:**

- [Jamming Equipment](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#jamming-equipment-ew-jammer): `range` = 600

### Active Missile Interference (`Electronic_Counters_Mk2`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/ECM_2.png -->

The introduction of more advanced missiles designed to defeat earlier electronic countermeasures like simple chaff and flares necessitated even better protection systems. Using active targeted radar and infrared emitters, it's possible to actively manipulate and confuse the guidance system of an enemy homing missile and mislead it into missing its target. Sudden randomized pulses of energy are sent out and received by the enemy weapon's seeker head, giving it conflicting information about the location and vector of its target which disorients it and may cause it to miss. Enemy missiles that are directly guided by their launcher can also be actively jammed, and if they are unable to receive commands and correct their course, they are very unlikely to hit their mark. Although chaff and flares are still useful, directly and actively attempting to interfere with the guidance system of a weapon is even more likely to fool it and foil an attack.  
Passively reduces the accuracy and damage done by enemy AShMs, SAMs and AAMs.

*Level 3, cost 20, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 4*

**Requires:** [Chaff and Flare Countermeasures](#chaff-and-flare-countermeasures-electronic_counters_mk1).

**Leads to:** [Advanced Chaff and IR Decoys](#advanced-chaff-and-ir-decoys-electronic_counters_mk3), [Stealth Technology](#stealth-technology-stealth_technology).

### Advanced Chaff and IR Decoys (`Electronic_Counters_Mk3`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/ECM_3.png -->

As missile guidance became smarter and smarter, electronic countermeasures would have to become more sophisticated and illusive to successfully confuse enemy missiles and throw them off course. Improvements to the shape and composition of chaff would allow it to linger in the air for longer periods of time and produce greater, more confusing radar returns. Additionally, broadcasting a jamming signal directly into a cloud of chaff can create a large, convincing false target for missiles. New countermeasure patterns and dispersion strategies were also developed to defeat tracking algorithms, by being more random and harder to discern, as well as attempting to fool the guidance system in a number of ways at multiple points along its course. Countermeasures for other types of guidance other than just infrared and radar would also be developed, and would evolve to a point where even relatively small systems could be protected, diminishing the effectiveness of enemy homing missiles as a whole. Although some missiles will still be able to bypass these countermeasures, advanced ECM will greatly improve the survivability of units threatened by guided missiles.  
Passively reduces the accuracy and damage done by enemy homing missiles, including those used against armies and combat engineers.

*Level 5, cost 25, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 6*

**Requires:** [Active Missile Interference](#active-missile-interference-electronic_counters_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Seeker Blinding Lasers](#seeker-blinding-lasers-electronic_counters_mk4).

### DRFM Jamming Systems (`Jammers_Mk3`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/EWF1_2.png -->

Digital Radio Frequency Memory Jamming, or DRFM jamming, is a jamming technique that involves cloning an enemy radar signal in order to mislead the radar transmitting it. In DRFM jamming, the opposing radar sends a radar signal to the jammer, which digitally 'memorizes' that radar signal. With the jammer now having knowledge of the enemy radar signal, it can accurately retransmit that signal back to the enemy and modulate it so that the enemy radar receives false information. Because the transmitted signal closely matches that which the radar put out, the radar can't discern between which information is real and which is the result of jamming, making DRFM jamming harder to recognize and harder to overcome. In essence, DRFM jamming allows jammers to fool enemy radars by broadcasting their own signal back at them, but modifying it slightly in order to mislead them and present them with false targeting information, making it more effective at fooling enemy radar than older techniques.  
Increases the effective range of jammers on enemy radar systems.

*Level 5, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 6*

**Requires:** [SEAD/EW Aircraft](#seadew-aircraft-jammers_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Advanced AESA Jammers](#advanced-aesa-jammers-jammers_mk4).

**Improves:**

- [Jamming Equipment](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#jamming-equipment-ew-jammer): `range` = 900

### Stealth Technology (`Stealth_Technology`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/STEALTH.png -->

Although the concept of 'stealth' in the traditional sense greatly predates human history, in the recent military context, it refers to a suite of features that render an object or a system difficult to observe or track using conventional methods like radar or thermal imaging. Stealth technology generally relies on radar-absorbing material, a distorting profile that minimizes radar returns and a number of other features designed to stop the object in question from reflecting radar and revealing its position. In military applications, this has obvious benefits as it makes the user much more difficult to find and destroy, and ensures they can keep the element of surprise for much longer.  
Basic stealth technology is a prerequisite to build stealth bombers and 5th generation aircraft.

*Level 5, cost 40, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 6*

**Requires:** [SEAD/EW Aircraft](#seadew-aircraft-jammers_mk2), [Active Missile Interference](#active-missile-interference-electronic_counters_mk2).

**Leads to:** [Stealth Bombers](#stealth-bombers-generation_5_bomber), [5th Generation Aircraft](#5th-generation-aircraft-generation_5_aircraft), [Novel Stealth Applications](#novel-stealth-applications-novel_stealth_applications).

### Novel Stealth Applications (`Novel_Stealth_Applications`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/NOVEL_STEALTH.png -->

Although it made its debut on combat aircraft, stealth technology has many potential military applications for both offensive and defencive purposes. Stealth technology can be applied to satellites and naval vessels in order to make them harder to detect and engage, which can be a huge advantage in avoiding unwanted encounters or making the first strike, both of which are crucial in the environments they operate in.  
Satellites gain stealth characteristics and are now harder to detect. Allows for the development of cutting-edge stealth warships.

*Level 7, cost 60*

**Requires:** [Stealth Technology](#stealth-technology-stealth_technology).

**Leads to:** [Stealth Weaponry](#stealth-weaponry-stealth_weapons), [Advanced Stealth](#advanced-stealth-advanced_stealth), [Experimental Stealth Warships](#experimental-stealth-warships-experimental_warships).

**Improves:**

- [Spy Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spy-satellite-satellite):
    - new appearance
    - `size` ×0.99
- [Launch Warning Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#launch-warning-satellite-early_warning_satellite), [Kinetic Strike Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#kinetic-strike-satellite-kinetic_strike_satellite), [Laser Defence Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#laser-defence-satellite-laser_defence_satellite), [Hunter-Killer Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#hunter-killer-satellite-hunter-killer_satellite), [Counterforce Laser Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#counterforce-laser-satellite-countervalue_laser_satellite): `size` ×0.99

### Stealth Weaponry (`Stealth_Weapons`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/STEALTH_WEAPONS.png -->

Stealth technology can be applied to missiles, to give their targets less warning of their approach, and therefore less time to react and take the appropriate action. As a result, stealth munitions are considerably harder to successfully evade or intercept, and may occasionally score kills without being noticed at all until they've already destroyed their target. Compared to other systems, weapons that incorporate stealth tend to be expensive and complex, but can actually be more economical against more defensible targets since they are also considerably more likely to take them by surprise, bypass their missile defences and successfully make contact.  
Increases the odds of a variety of missiles successfully hitting their targets, and reduces their odds of being intercepted.

*Level 7, cost 60*

**Requires:** [Novel Stealth Applications](#novel-stealth-applications-novel_stealth_applications).

**Improves:**

- [AShM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#ashm-anti-ship-missile), [SSM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#ssm-ssm), [Nuclear SSM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#nuclear-ssm-nuclear-cruise-missile), [Fusion SSM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#fusion-ssm-fusion-cruise-missile), [AGM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#agm-agm), [Helicopter ATGM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#helicopter-atgm-helicopter-atgm), [Anti-Radar Missile](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#anti-radar-missile-anti-radiation-missile), [Light AShM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#light-ashm-light_ashm), [ALCM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#alcm-alcm), [Nuclear ALCM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#nuclear-alcm-alcm-n), [Pure Fusion ALCM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#pure-fusion-alcm-fusion-alcm): `canbeintercepted` ×0.8
- [AAM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#aam-aam), [LAAM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#laam-laam), [Nuclear AAM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#nuclear-aam-nuclear-aam), [Pure-Fusion AAM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#pure-fusion-aam-fusion-aam), [Nuclear SAM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#nuclear-sam-nuclear-sam), [Pure Fusion SAM](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#pure-fusion-sam-fusion-sam): `precision` ×0.8

### Advanced AESA Jammers (`Jammers_Mk4`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/EWF1.png -->

Jamming weapons that use Active Electronically Scanned Array (AESA) transmitters are capable of targeting their jamming more directly than older systems and have the ability to jam on a wider spectrum of frequencies at once, making them more effective at overwhelming enemy radar while diminishing the effectiveness of the enemy's jamming countermeasures. Combined with improved electronics, better jamming techniques and other yet undisclosed technology, next generation jammers will be even more powerful and more capable of attacking a wider spectrum of frequencies at once, making them very difficult to counteract and resist by all but the most advanced and discerning radar systems.  
Further increase the effective range of jammers.

*Level 8, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 10*

**Requires:** [AESA Radar](#aesa-radar-radar_mk4), [DRFM Jamming Systems](#drfm-jamming-systems-jammers_mk3).

**Improves:**

- [Jamming Equipment](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#jamming-equipment-ew-jammer): `range` = 1 200

### Seeker Blinding Lasers (`Electronic_Counters_Mk4`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/ECM_4.png -->

Sufficiently powerful lasers are capable of blinding the guidance systems on optically-guided missiles, as well as heating up the electrical components on non-optical weapons to a point where they overheat and stop functioning properly. Lasers that are powerful enough to blind or disable (but not destroy) incoming missiles have the potential to offer a relatively lightweight anti-missile active protection system for small platforms like combat aircraft. Although it's not a perfect defence and these weapons may not be able to always dazzle enemy missiles, a successfully damaged and blinded missile is far, far less likely to score a kill than one that's in working order. Combining active blinding with other passive methods like chaff and flares will make it much harder for incoming missiles to hit their mark, but it doesn't guarantee immunity, especially against multiple sufficiently advanced weapons.  
Passively reduces the accuracy and damage done by enemy homing missiles, including those used against armies and combat engineers.

*Level 8, cost 35, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 10*

**Requires:** [Advanced Laser Guidance](#advanced-laser-guidance-advanced_laser_guidance), [Advanced Chaff and IR Decoys](#advanced-chaff-and-ir-decoys-electronic_counters_mk3).

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/STEALTH_ADVANCED.png -->

Systems that can take advantage of these advancements can expect to be even more difficult to detect, and by extension, easier to protect and more dangerous when on the attack.

## Satellites and Space Weapons

### Launch Warning Satellites (`Launch_Warning_Satellite`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SATELLITE_EWS.png -->

Because of their unhindered sightlines and global coverage, satellites naturally became ideal platforms for detecting nuclear explosions and high-altitude missile launches. Using infrared imaging, early warning satellites can detect the thermal signatures of rocket exhaust plumes and then relay the information to a command center. However, their detection range is limited by their infrared optics and they can be targeted by anti-satellite weapons.  
Unlocks Launch Warning Satellites, which can detect ballistic missiles over a wide area.

*Level 2, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 3*

**Requires:** [Spy Satellites](#spy-satellites-satellite).

**Leads to:** [Improved Launch Warning Satellites](#improved-launch-warning-satellites-improved_launch_warning_satellite).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Launch Warning Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#launch-warning-satellite-early_warning_satellite) (`Early_Warning_Satellite`)

### Spy Satellites (`Satellite`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPY_SATELLITE_1.png -->

After the first manmade objects left the atmosphere and began to orbit, it wasn’t long before plans to exploit outer space as a strategic asset were realized. Spy satellites were developed to orbit over enemy territory and gather intelligence, initially by taking extremely high-altitude photographs. Early spy satellites produced low-quality photos on film that had to be dropped from space and manually retrieved, making it a cumbersome and extremely expensive process that offered information that was difficult to interpret. Still, spy satellites were invaluable as they could see what nobody else could, and could identify major enemy installations with little risk.  
Satellites orbit around the planet in a fixed path and can only be attacked with specialized anti-satellite weapons, making them much less vulnerable but much less controllable than other systems. Early spy satellites will only detect land units, do not have the reaction-time needed to reliably report naval units and will not provide accurate targeting information.  
Spy satellites and other space assets will need a Space Center in order to be built, and will also need Satellite Relay Centers to operate effectively.

*Level 2, cost 50, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 2*

**Requires:** nothing.

**Leads to:** [Launch Warning Satellites](#launch-warning-satellites-launch_warning_satellite), [Electro-Optical Data-linked Satellites](#electro-optical-data-linked-satellites-improved_optics), [Nuclear Anti-Satellite Weapons](#nuclear-anti-satellite-weapons-nuke_asat), [Orbital Attack Satellites](#orbital-attack-satellites-orbital_attack_satellite).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Space Center](A1-Catalog-Units.md#space-center-space_operations_center) (`Space_Operations_Center`)
- Unit [Satellite Relay Center](A1-Catalog-Units.md#satellite-relay-center-satellite_relay_station) (`Satellite_Relay_Station`)
- Unit [Spy Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spy-satellite-satellite) (`Satellite`)

### Nuclear Anti-Satellite Weapons (`Nuke_ASAT`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/NUKE_ASAT.png -->

Being able to defeat satellites can provide a considerable advantage against a technologically advanced enemy. However, early anti-satellite (ASAT) weapons lacked the precision to have any remote chance of hitting a satellite or detonating close enough to damage one with conventional explosives. As a consequence, early ASAT weapons had to be equipped with nuclear warheads in order to be able to destroy or even damage their targets. Because of the nature of ASAT weapons and their requirement to explode at high altitude, nuclear ASATs are prone to generating EMPs, making them very efficient at disabling enemy satellites but also prone to extreme levels of collateral damage and friendly fire.  
Unlocks nuclear silo-launched ASATs, which are best used against enemies that have a clear advantage in their control over space. Nuclear ASATs are liable to generate EMPs and destroy friendly satellites, so use them with discretion!

*Level 3, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 4*

**Requires:** [Object Cataloging Space Radar](#object-cataloging-space-radar-space_radar), [Improved Missile Guidance](#improved-missile-guidance-improved_missile_guidance), [Spy Satellites](#spy-satellites-satellite).

**Leads to:** [Precision ASAT Weapons](#precision-asat-weapons-asat).

**Unlocks:**

- Weapon [Nuclear ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#nuclear-asat-nuclear-asat) (`Nuclear ASAT`)

### Electro-Optical Data-linked Satellites (`Improved_optics`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPY_SATELLITE_2.png -->

As space launch capabilities and telecommunications improved, it became possible to design heavier spy satellites capable of actively transmitting data to a control center at a rate with practical applications. At the same time, the advent of electro-optical digital imaging meant that it was possible to send footage digitally as a stream of data, rather than in a physical format like a film canister. Combining these two technologies, spy satellites would be able to take footage of enemy assets and digitally relay it to a receiver, greatly simplifying and streamlining the intelligence gathering process and making it much faster. As a result, spy satellites could provide relevant information on time-sensitive targets like naval assets, and also more-readily survey static assets as well.  
Increases the observation range for spy satellites.  
Allows spy satellites to identify naval assets.

*Level 3, cost 45, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 4*

**Requires:** [Spy Satellites](#spy-satellites-satellite).

**Leads to:** [Real-Time HD Satellites](#real-time-hd-satellites-advanced_sat_optics), [Laser Defence Satellites](#laser-defence-satellites-satellite_defence_lasers), [Orbital Attack Satellites](#orbital-attack-satellites-orbital_attack_satellite), [Counterforce DEW Satellite](#counterforce-dew-satellite-direct_energy_satellite).

**Improves:**

- [Satellite Relay Center](A1-Catalog-Units.md#satellite-relay-center-satellite_relay_station): new appearance
- [Spy Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spy-satellite-satellite):
    - new appearance
    - sensors become:
        - [Digital Spy Camera](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#digital-spy-camera-datalink-satellite-camera)
        - [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite Optics Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-optics-display-satellite-optics-display)
- [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite Optics Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-optics-display-satellite-optics-display): `range` = 600

### Improved Launch Warning Satellites (`Improved_Launch_Warning_Satellite`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SATELLITE_EWS_2.png -->

Improved infrared camera systems with increased sensitivity and fields of view, plus more advanced computing systems to match, will result in early warning satellites that can pick up thermal signatures from greater distances and relay the information faster, meaning fewer satellites can provide more coverage, and incoming missile threats are less likely to go unnoticed before it’s too late.  
Improves the detection range of launch warning satellites.

*Level 4, cost 30, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 5*

**Requires:** [Launch Warning Satellites](#launch-warning-satellites-launch_warning_satellite).

**Leads to:** [Advanced Launch Warning Satellites](#advanced-launch-warning-satellites-advanced_launch_warning_satellite).

**Improves:**

- [Launch Warning Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#launch-warning-satellite-early_warning_satellite): new appearance
- [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite EWS Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-ews-display-satellite-ews-display): `range` = 4 000

### Precision ASAT Weapons (`ASAT`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/ASAT.png -->

Although nuclear-armed ASATs were undoubtedly capable of destroying their targets, their propensity to completely wipe out nearby satellite constellations and electrical power grids was deemed to be a little excessive. As a result, once guided missile technology was sufficiently advanced, new conventional precision ASAT weapons were developed, which could target individual satellites without causing catastrophic collateral damage. Although the residual debris caused by these weapons still poses a risk to other satellites and can potentially start an ablation cascade (i.e. Kessler Syndrome), they are far more discretionary than earlier nuclear-armed designs, generally making them more suitable for most applications.  
Unlocks conventional direct-ascent ASATs which are fired from missile silos, AL-ASATs which are fired from interceptors, and co-orbital ASATs, which are launched from silos and converge on their targets slowly over long distances.

*Level 5, cost 60, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 6*

**Requires:** [Nuclear Anti-Satellite Weapons](#nuclear-anti-satellite-weapons-nuke_asat), [Enhanced Missile Guidance](#enhanced-missile-guidance-enhanced_missile_guidance).

**Leads to:** [Space-To-Space Weapons](#space-to-space-weapons-space_to_space_weapons), [Advanced ASAT Weapons](#advanced-asat-weapons-advanced_asat).

**Unlocks:**

- Weapon [ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#asat-asat) (`ASAT`)
- Weapon [Air-Launched ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#air-launched-asat-aircraft-asat) (`Aircraft ASAT`)
- Weapon [Co-Orbital ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#co-orbital-asat-co-orbital-asat) (`Co-Orbital ASAT`)

**Improves:**

- [Nuclear ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#nuclear-asat-nuclear-asat):
    - `range` = 3 000
    - `speed` = 9 000

### Real-Time HD Satellites (`Advanced_Sat_Optics`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPY_SATELLITE_3.png -->

High-speed satellite communications are useful for broadcasting television or helping with commercial navigation, and it's little surprise it has military applications as well. Real-time, high-speed satellite communications allow spy satellites to rapidly send encrypted data to their receiving stations, allowing them to broadcast the intelligence they're gathering almost in real-time. As a result, spy satellites can effectively track highly time-sensitive targets like aircraft in flight, and also provide accurate, up-to-date targeting data for certain precision weapons. With the addition of better, higher-resolution cameras capable of picking up ever-finer details, sufficiently advanced spy satellites can monitor huge swathes of the globe, and report their findings almost instantly, provided there's a receiver to listen to them. Software and computing improvements will also streamline the process of sorting through the incoming data and identifying relevant contacts.  
Further increases the observation range of spy satellites.  
Spy satellites can now track aircraft and large high-altitude missiles.  
Spy satellites can now provide targeting data for certain precision weapons.

*Level 6, cost 60, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 8*

**Requires:** [Electro-Optical Data-linked Satellites](#electro-optical-data-linked-satellites-improved_optics).

**Leads to:** [Ultra-Resolution Streaming Satellites](#ultra-resolution-streaming-satellites-experimental_sat_optics), [Infantry Orbital Drop Capsules](#infantry-orbital-drop-capsules-infantry_orbital_drop_capsules).

**Improves:**

- [Spy Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spy-satellite-satellite):
    - sensors become:
        - [HD Spy Camera](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#hd-spy-camera-high-resolution-satellite-camera)
        - [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite Optics Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-optics-display-satellite-optics-display)
- [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite Optics Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-optics-display-satellite-optics-display): `range` = 1 200

### Advanced Launch Warning Satellites (`Advanced_Launch_Warning_Satellite`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SATELLITE_EWS_3.png -->

Advanced launch warning satellites can detect relatively minor thermal signatures, analyse their characteristics, determine their distance from the satellite to evaluate their intensity, and use that information to identify missile launches. As a result, even relatively small flare-ups, which may in truth be ballistic missile launches, can be flagged and analysed further, allowing these new satellites to potentially identify airborne ballistic missile threats within seconds of them entering their line-of-sight.  
Further improves the detection range of launch warning satellites.

*Level 7, cost 45, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 8*

**Requires:** [Improved Launch Warning Satellites](#improved-launch-warning-satellites-improved_launch_warning_satellite).

**Improves:**

- [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite EWS Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-ews-display-satellite-ews-display): `range` = 7 250

### Space-To-Space Weapons (`Space_to_Space_Weapons`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SAT_TO_SAT.png -->

The proliferation of weapons in outer-space would naturally reach a logical conclusion where certain space-borne assets would be created with the sole intention of destroying other space-borne assets. Early concepts for armed satellites had basic anti-aircraft guns as a means of defence, but in an era of advanced weaponry and space warfare, high-powered lasers and beyond-line-of-sight ASAT missiles are much more practical and effective. Equipping laser-armed satellites with the systems needed to locate and destroy enemy satellites would make space a much more dangerous environment, and the development of specialized 'hunter-killer' satellites designed to secure space-superiority through active fighting, would only make it even more hostile.  
Allows laser defence satellites to attack other satellites.  
Unlocks Hunter-Killer Satellites, which have powerful lasers, are designed to better withstand ASAT attacks, and are armed with long-range ASAT missiles.

*Level 7, cost 60, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 99*

**Requires:** [Precision ASAT Weapons](#precision-asat-weapons-asat), [Laser Defence Satellites](#laser-defence-satellites-satellite_defence_lasers).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Hunter-Killer Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#hunter-killer-satellite-hunter-killer_satellite) (`Hunter-Killer_Satellite`)
- Weapon [Satellite Launched ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#satellite-launched-asat-satellite-asat) (`Satellite ASAT`)
- Weapon [Satellite ASAT Laser](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#satellite-asat-laser-asat-laser) (`ASAT Laser`)

### Laser Defence Satellites (`Satellite_Defence_Lasers`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/LASER_SAT_DEF.png -->

Ground-based missile defences have a number of shortcomings, namely their limited range and window to engage incoming missiles, and per unit costs. As a result, eventually new concepts were created for missile defence systems. One such concept was a constellation of satellites armed with high-powered laser weapons, that would shoot down incoming missiles while they were still in space. This hypothetical system had advantages in that it could engage missiles in the midcourse and boost phases of their trajectories, well before they were close to hitting their targets. Because these lasers would operate in space, they would require less energy to be effective since they would not be diminished by the atmosphere. Still, a sufficiently powerful laser weapon would need to exist to reliably disable ballistic warheads. These laser systems also had an advantage in that they could hypothetically counter ASAT missiles as well, or be used to attack enemy satellites, though tracking and targeting those satellites would be another challenge.  
Unlocks Laser Defence Satellites, which will shoot down enemy warheads and ASAT weapons in space. The weapons on these satellites can also be repurposed to attack enemy satellites given the appropriate upgrades.

*Level 7, cost 90, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 99*

**Requires:** [Object Cataloging Space Radar](#object-cataloging-space-radar-space_radar), [Electro-Optical Data-linked Satellites](#electro-optical-data-linked-satellites-improved_optics), [High-Powered Lasers](#high-powered-lasers-high_powered_lasers).

**Leads to:** [Space-To-Space Weapons](#space-to-space-weapons-space_to_space_weapons).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Laser Defence Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#laser-defence-satellite-laser_defence_satellite) (`Laser_Defence_Satellite`)
- Weapon [Missile Defence Laser](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#missile-defence-laser-satellite-laser) (`Satellite Laser`)

### Advanced ASAT Weapons (`Advanced_ASAT`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/ASAT_2.png -->

With the proliferation of ASAT weapons and the continuously growing role that space assets play in both civil and military operations, having access to sophisticated anti-satellite capabilities is becoming more and more desirable as time goes on. It's been suggested that the most advanced ASAT weapons are hypothetically capable of destroying targets as far out as geostationary orbit, which would mean they could hypothetically target military GPS satellites. Regardless of what their hypothetical capabilities are, more advanced ASAT weapons can be expected to perform considerably better than the first conventional examples developed decades earlier, allowing them to more reliably defeat enemy satellites more quickly and from further away, as well as counteract emergent anti-ASAT countermeasures.  
Unlocks Satellite-Fired ASATs, which are used by Hunter-Killer Satellites.  
Increases the range, accuracy and damage of ASAT missiles and makes them harder to intercept.

*Level 8, cost 60, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 10*

**Requires:** [Precision ASAT Weapons](#precision-asat-weapons-asat), [Advanced Missile Guidance](#advanced-missile-guidance-advanced_missile_guidance).

**Unlocks:**

- Weapon [Satellite Launched ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#satellite-launched-asat-satellite-asat) (`Satellite ASAT`)

**Improves:**

- [ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#asat-asat):
    - `range` = 4 500
    - `canbeintercepted` ×0.75
    - `precision` ×0.5
    - `damage` ×1.5
- [Air-Launched ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#air-launched-asat-aircraft-asat):
    - `range` = 2 500
    - `canbeintercepted` ×0.75
    - `precision` ×0.5
    - `damage` ×1.5
- [Co-Orbital ASAT](A2-Catalog-Missiles.md#co-orbital-asat-co-orbital-asat):
    - `speed` = 6 500
    - `canbeintercepted` ×0.75
    - `precision` ×0.5
    - `damage` ×1.5

### Ultra-Resolution Streaming Satellites (`Experimental_Sat_Optics`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/SPY_SATELLITE_4.png -->

Explosive leaps forward in optical, datalink and computing technology will make it possible for the most advanced spy satellites to identify and track dozens or hundreds of significant objects over a huge area and relay their positions to a control center in real time. State of the art optical systems can capture images in extremely high detail over hundreds of kilometers and provide access to a massive wealth of raw visual data. Using highly sophisticated computers with massive amounts of processing power and AI-driven algorithms, that data can quickly be analysed to find enemy units and be turned into actionable intelligence. In essence, these highly advanced satellites and their control centers will be able to actively track objects as small as light cruise missiles over a huge region of the globe in real time, and gather and sort through photos of thousands of square kilometers of land to root out enemy activity wherever it can be located.  
Greatly increases the observation range of spy satellites.  
Spy satellites can now see small low-altitude missiles.

*Level 8, cost 75, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 10*

**Requires:** [Real-Time HD Satellites](#real-time-hd-satellites-advanced_sat_optics).

**Improves:**

- [Spy Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spy-satellite-satellite):
    - sensors become:
        - [Ultra-HD Spy Camera](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#ultra-hd-spy-camera-ultra-resolution-satellite-camera)
        - [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite Optics Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-optics-display-satellite-optics-display)
- [Maximum Optical Range (Satellite Optics Display)](A4-Catalog-Radars.md#maximum-optical-range-satellite-optics-display-satellite-optics-display): `range` = 1 750

### Orbital Attack Satellites (`Orbital_Attack_Satellite`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/Attack_Satellite.png -->

Satellites have great potential to be used as weapon platforms. Due to their location in orbit, they are impossible to attack without specialized weaponry and can freely attack targets along their orbital path. Because of their extreme speed and elevation, they can do substantial damage simply by dropping inert kinetic projectiles that crash into the ground with the same destructive force as a very small nuclear weapon. The main engineering challenges posed by such a system would be getting such a heavy payload into orbit, as well as developing a system that could deploy and brake the kinetic payloads precisely enough for them to hit their targets with sufficient accuracy.  
Unlocks Kinetic Strike Satellites, which can attack ground targets along their orbital path.

*Level 8, cost 135, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 99*

**Requires:** [Spy Satellites](#spy-satellites-satellite), [Electro-Optical Data-linked Satellites](#electro-optical-data-linked-satellites-improved_optics).

**Leads to:** [Counterforce DEW Satellite](#counterforce-dew-satellite-direct_energy_satellite).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Kinetic Strike Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#kinetic-strike-satellite-kinetic_strike_satellite) (`Kinetic_Strike_Satellite`)

### Infantry Orbital Drop Capsules (`Infantry_Orbital_Drop_Capsules`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/DROP_POD.png -->

Shortly after astronauts safety returned from space in reentry capsules for the first time, military planners were quick to conceptualize a system where military forces could be launched into space and then be rapidly deployed anywhere in the world within hours, by dropping from orbit in a capsule just like astronauts. At conception, this was a flawed and implausible concept as there were major issues with payload capacity, safety (i.e. crashing a pod full of ammunition and explosives into the ground), reentry accuracy, training requirements, and of course, the extreme cost associated with space launches. However, with spaceflight technology becoming cheaper, more accessible and more capable, it may become feasible and practical to deploy elite special forces from orbit. Of course, a reentry capsule careerning into enemy territory from space would be extremely noticable and ruin any element of secrecy, but the flexibility and surprise afforded by being able to deploy special forces anywhere in the world certainly has some strategic merit.  
Unlocks Military Space Shuttles, which can deploy special forces over long distances.  
Space Shuttles are operated by Space Centers, can only be detected by Space Radars and can only be attacked by ASAT weapons.

*Level 10, cost 100, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 99*

**Requires:** [Real-Time HD Satellites](#real-time-hd-satellites-advanced_sat_optics).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Space Shuttle](A1-Catalog-Units.md#space-shuttle-space_shuttle) (`Space_Shuttle`)

### Counterforce DEW Satellite (`Direct_Energy_Satellite`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/DEW_Laser_Sat.png -->

A sufficiently powerful laser could hypothetically be used to destroy strategic targets like launch sites, buildings or warships, but a major problem with laser weapons is that they require a line of sight to the target in order to engage it. Putting the system in orbit solves this problem as it now has unhampered sightlines, but penetrating the atmosphere and sufficiently irradiating the target requires a massively powerful laser and a very potent energy source. Nuclear power would be the only viable option to operate such a system, and the laser itself would have to be extremely sophisticated and capable of surviving its own power. As a result, such a system would be prohibitingly expensive to develop, build and launch into orbit, but could theoretically offer unmatched counterforce capabilities, provided it could be kept protected.  
Unlocks the counterforce laser satellite, which is extremely expensive but can destroy an unlimited number of ground targets along its orbital path as long as it has targeting data.

*Level 10, cost 140, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 99*

**Requires:** [Electro-Optical Data-linked Satellites](#electro-optical-data-linked-satellites-improved_optics), [Orbital Attack Satellites](#orbital-attack-satellites-orbital_attack_satellite), [Deadly Laser Weapons](#deadly-laser-weapons-offensive_laser_weapons).

**Unlocks:**

- Unit [Counterforce Laser Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#counterforce-laser-satellite-countervalue_laser_satellite) (`Countervalue_Laser_Satellite`)

## Domestic Technologies

### Basic EMP Shielding (`EMP_Defence`)

<!-- img:tech Images/Science/EMP_DEFENCE_1.png -->

When a nuclear weapon explodes, it creates an electromagnetic pulse, or EMP, which can disrupt electronic equipment. The EMP effects of nuclear weapons were predicted even before the very first tests were performed, and in fact the equipment at those tests were shielded accordingly. Electronics can be shielded from EMPs by either encasing them with a metal electrical shield to absorb the current, or by hardening the individual components in the electronics that are most likely to be damaged. Protecting the most crucial and vulnerable military electronics from EMPs will minimize the downtime spent repairing them and allow many of them to keep operating, albeit at an impaired capacity. Civilian assets, however, will still be extremely vulnerable.  
Without this technology, many units will be completely stunned when attacked by an EMP and aircraft will crash instantly. Be sure to invest in this if you ever suspect your enemies will develop EMP weapons!  
Reduces the severity and duration of EMP effects on military hardware.

*Level 1, cost 10, `NoAutoUnlockTillLevel` 3*

**Requires:** nothing.

**Leads to:** [Comprehensive EMP Shielding](#comprehensive-emp-shielding-emp_defence_mk2).

**Improves:**

- [Fighter](A1-Catalog-Units.md#fighter-fighter), [Multirole Attacker](A1-Catalog-Units.md#multirole-attacker-attack), [Interceptor](A1-Catalog-Units.md#interceptor-interceptor), [Strategic Bomber](A1-Catalog-Units.md#strategic-bomber-bomber), [High-Speed Bomber](A1-Catalog-Units.md#high-speed-bomber-high_speed_bomber), [AWACS](A1-Catalog-Units.md#awacs-awacs), [Carrier AWACS](A1-Catalog-Units.md#carrier-awacs-naval-awacs), [Spy Plane](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spy-plane-spy_plane), [Naval Helicopter](A1-Catalog-Units.md#naval-helicopter-navy_helicopter), [Spec-Ops Helicopter](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spec-ops-helicopter-spec_ops_helicopter), [Special Mission Aircraft](A1-Catalog-Units.md#special-mission-aircraft-sof_aircraft), [Attack Helicopter](A1-Catalog-Units.md#attack-helicopter-attack_helicopter), [EW Aircraft](A1-Catalog-Units.md#ew-aircraft-ew_aircraft), [Air Transport](A1-Catalog-Units.md#air-transport-air_transport), [Spy Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#spy-satellite-satellite), [Launch Warning Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#launch-warning-satellite-early_warning_satellite), [Kinetic Strike Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#kinetic-strike-satellite-kinetic_strike_satellite), [Laser Defence Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#laser-defence-satellite-laser_defence_satellite), [Hunter-Killer Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#hunter-killer-satellite-hunter-killer_satellite), [Counterforce Laser Satellite](A1-Catalog-Units.md#counterforce-laser-satellite-countervalue_laser_satellite): loses the [“EMP Killable”](A1-Catalog-Units.md#emp-killable-emp_killable) (`EMP_Killable`) trait
